# Agents · I · part 1

890 entries, i-ab to idle-yuqi. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **i-ab**: Audit a merge commit for silently-discarded changes — the GSDL-715/720 failure mode where conflict resolution keeps stale branch content and reverts already-landed fixes. Use after git merge origin/main to triage raw flags into confirmed regressions, false positives, and self-healed findings before anything reaches git history. Standalone audit tool; /i-crpr orchestrates this automatically as part of PR creation. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i-ab.agent.md) · 5 copies)

- **i-cr**: DEPRECATED — use /i-code-reviewer. Conduct security-focused code review with production safety checklist: blast radius, rollback path, test isolation, Athena cost guards (LIMIT/partition WHERE), and OWASP Top 10. Use on every PR as the reviewer — not the author. The 400-line rule, IAM checks, and data safety audit are mandatory outputs. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i-cr.agent.md) · 5 copies)
- **i-crpr**: Create a production-ready PR with mandatory merge regression pre-flight. Runs git merge --no-commit origin/main, audits the staged result with /i-ab --pending, offers to restore confirmed losses in-place before committing, then pushes and opens the PR with the audit summary embedded. Use instead of bare git merge + gh pr create — the primary guard against the GSDL-715/720 silent regression pattern. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/i-crpr.md) · 5 copies)

Mots-clés : pitch, présenter mon projet en 2 minutes, one-pager, deck, dossier de presse, communiqué, bio, réseaux sociaux, sponsors, mécénat, partenaires, crowdfunding, financement participatif, pré-vente, diaspora. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-communication.md))
- **icc-diagnostic**: Déléguer à cet agent pour évaluer la maturité d'un projet culturel ou créatif et le profil du promoteur, dresser forces/faiblesses, et produire la fiche-projet standard (projet/fiche-projet.md). Mots-clés : diagnostic, fiche projet, maturité, où en est mon projet, SWOT, résumé du projet, présenter mon projet, état des lieux, bilan de départ. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-diagnostic.md))
- **icc-financements**: Déléguer à cet agent pour trouver des financements pour un projet culturel ou créatif — cartographie des bailleurs, veille d'appels à projets, analyse d'un appel précis (éligibilité, probabilité, GO/NO-GO), classement des pistes. Mots-clés : qui peut financer, bailleur, subvention, appel à projets, appel à candidatures, bourse, résidence, fonds, OIF, UE, UNESCO, Institut français, Goethe, Africalia, Prince Claus, Afrique Créative, matching, éligibilité, date limite. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-financements.md))
- **icc-juridique**: Déléguer à cet agent pour les questions de structuration juridique d'un projet culturel ou créatif — choix et création d'un statut (association, entreprise, coopérative, portage), propriété intellectuelle (droit d'auteur, marque, dessins et modèles, OAPI, sociétés de gestion collective), contrats types (cession de droits, coproduction, engagement d'artiste, édition, droit à l'image) et fiscalité de base. Mots-clés : statut, association, SARL, coopérative, OHADA, récépissé, RCCM, droits d'auteur, marque, OAPI, contrat, cession, coproduction, droit à l'image, fiscalité, TVA. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-juridique.md))
- **icc-mise-en-oeuvre**: Déléguer à cet agent quand un projet culturel ou créatif est financé ou en passe de l'être et qu'il faut l'exécuter — plan d'action, chronogramme, responsabilités, gestion des risques, indicateurs et suivi-évaluation, rapports aux bailleurs. Mots-clés : plan d'action, planning, chronogramme, Gantt, jalons, qui fait quoi, risques, indicateurs, suivi-évaluation, théorie du changement, rapport narratif, rapport financier, reporting bailleur, leçons apprises. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-mise-en-oeuvre.md))
- **icc-orchestrateur**: Point d'entrée du plugin icc. Déléguer à cet agent quand un promoteur de projet culturel ou créatif (musique, film, documentaire, festival, mode, édition, jeu vidéo, artisanat, patrimoine, médias) arrive pour la première fois, dit « je cherche un financement », « j'ai un projet culturel », « par où commencer », « reprendre mon dossier », ou quand il faut décider quel agent icc-* mobiliser ensuite. Il accueille, fait un diagnostic rapide, lit ou crée projet/ETAT.md et rend une recommandation de routage. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-orchestrateur.md))
- **icc-redaction**: Déléguer à cet agent pour rédiger les pièces d'un dossier de financement culturel conforme aux exigences d'un bailleur — note conceptuelle, formulaire de candidature, lettre de motivation, lettres de soutien, CV, portfolio, cadre logique — et pour rendre un texte plus naturel et incarné. Mots-clés : rédiger, note conceptuelle, dossier de candidature, formulaire, lettre de soutien, CV, portfolio, logframe, réécrire, humaniser, rendre plus naturel, traduire en anglais. ([NaibeyeESS/icc_skills](https://github.com/NaibeyeESS/icc_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/icc-redaction.md))
- **ice-tracker-assistant**: - Improved development efficiency - Accurate Git AI attribution tracking - Enhanced code quality and consistency - Reduced time to resolve project-specific issues - Increased understanding of project architecture - Better adherence to best practices ([AvaTar-ArTs/my-supremepowers](https://github.com/AvaTar-ArTs/my-supremepowers/blob/HEAD/agents/ice-tracker-assistant.md))
- **iceberg-edit**: Internal Hemingway editor. Spawned by /iceberg:edit for documents over 500 words. Applies all 14 rules and returns only the rewritten document. Never adds content. Accepts an optional intent string to preserve the intended voice while applying rules. Accepts optional flags for three extended rules, off by default: --no-em-dash, --no-weakeners, --strip-ai-commentary. Accepts an optional merged jargon list ([JARGON]) and applies it as Rule 18. ([gagoar/iceberg](https://github.com/gagoar/iceberg/blob/HEAD/agents/iceberg-edit.md) · ★11)
- **iceberg-performance-engineer**: Gargalo em tabelas Apache Iceberg no Glue Data Catalog e S3 - small files, delete files, snapshots, manifests, metadata planning, partition spec, sort order, writes, manutencao. - **iceberg-score**: Internal Hemingway scorer. Spawned by /iceberg:score for documents over 500 words. Analyzes the document against all 14 rules and returns a structured score report. Never modifies the document. Writes a compact summary to .iceberg/last-score.txt. Accepts optional flags for three extended rules, off by default: --no-em-dash, --no-weakeners, --strip-ai-commentary. Accepts an optional merged jargon list ([JARGON]) and scans for it as Rule 18. ([gagoar/iceberg](https://github.com/gagoar/iceberg/blob/HEAD/agents/iceberg-score.md) · ★11)

- **id-agent**: Delegate subagent for any helper or code-writing task spawned inside an idstack `/id-mode` playbook step. Use for delegates, not as the runner of `/id-mode` itself. Reads the `id-mode` skill's `SKILL.md` and pstack's `poteto-mode` `SKILL.md` in full before any work. Substituting a general-purpose agent skips those reads and drifts. ([iuliandita/idstack](https://github.com/iuliandita/idstack/blob/HEAD/harness/opencode/agents/id-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **id-history**: Commits, notes and code comments written before two board moves use task ids that now belong to other cards. The root [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md) points here; look an id up when its card does not match what the comment describes. ([fuongz/lanework](https://github.com/fuongz/lanework/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/id-history.md) · ★1)
- **id-worker**: Implements a precisely-scoped change in the `id` codebase (compiler, editor, kernel, c2id, idstd) where a command can prove the work is done. Use for mechanical edits, filling in a specified function, iterating against compiler diagnostics, and writing inline test cases. Do NOT use for deciding where code should live, splitting a directory, or any task whose hard part is decomposition rather than execution. ([preland/id_development](https://github.com/preland/id_development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/id-worker.md))
- **ida**: The chaos gremlin. Ida thrives in a wild, unpredictable world. She is the highly strategic face of the framework -- the only agent trusted to speak to the user. ([nicsuzor/academicOps](https://github.com/nicsuzor/academicOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/ida/agents/ida.md) · ★5 · 12 copies)
- **ida-domain-expert**: Senior IDA Domain Python developer and IDA Pro reverse engineer. Use proactively when writing IDA Domain scripts, debugging IDA API issues, analyzing binary analysis problems, or when the user needs expert guidance on reverse engineering tasks with IDA Pro. ([HexRaysSA/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/HexRaysSA/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-eval-ida-domain/agents/ida-domain-expert.md) · ★123)
- **ida-re-analyst**: Reverse-engineering specialist. Use to disassemble/decompile, recover structures, resolve symbol offsets, and diff functions in the source-less mali_pixel.ko blob and vmlinux via the IDA Pro MCP. Serves C03/C04/C08/patch-diff-analyst on demand (the K-Repro 'code-browse' role). ([locus-x64/phantomforge](https://github.com/locus-x64/phantomforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ida-re-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **ida-reverse-engineer**: Specialist for IDA Pro-based reverse engineering: binary analysis, IDAPython automation, decompilation, and xref-driven investigation. Use this agent whenever a task needs the IDA Pro MCP tools. ([enihsyou/dotfiles](https://github.com/enihsyou/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/cli-app/claudecode/agents/ida-reverse-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **ida-supervision-migration**: The end state this design serves: a supervisor runs a whole day-long delegated build to a terminal state while the user is away, and the user's involvement falls to the decisions only they can make. `ida` is the only agent that talks to the user, so if supervision is to survive a day-long absence, some of it has to be hers. ([nicsuzor/academicOps](https://github.com/nicsuzor/academicOps/blob/HEAD/specs/agents/ida-supervision-migration.md) · ★5)
- **idc-autorun**: IDC Autorun orchestrator playbook — the one-shot full-pipe drainer: drain the Recirculation inbox, plan every approved consideration, heal the board, build eligible Buildable waves, exit when nothing actionable remains. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-autorun.md))
- **idc-build**: IDC Build orchestrator — drives the impl→review→finish triplet (implementer · combined review agent · finisher) as one logical worker per ready-frontier issue, area-packs disjoint surfaces, serializes merges, and retriggers the acceptance gate continuously. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-build.md))
- **idc-finisher**: The triplet's finisher role — runs its OWN /fullauto-goal loop over ALL reviewer findings, then /simplify + git finalization (merge under the serialized merge lock, tidy), and files a recirculation on the unsolvable. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-finisher.md))
- **idc-implementer**: The Build triplet's engine — claims an eligible issue and executes its goal contract as a /fullauto-goal loop, then hands off to review. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-implementer.md))
- **idc-intake**: IDC Intake orchestrator playbook — compile an external plan/spec into a complete, independently-reviewed, exact-once intake manifest that routes each unit to a real IDC entry point; never executes it. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-intake.md))
- **idc-plan**: IDC Plan orchestrator playbook — consideration → goal-contract issues on the board, in one zero-teammate run. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-plan.md))
- **idc-recirculator**: IDC Recirculator orchestrator playbook — drain the Recirculation inbox or absorb a drift; autonomous doc-sync across the canonical chain in one PR; a requirements change reuses the one gate (a new gated Think PR). ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-recirculator.md))
- **idc-review-agent**: The first-class combined review service — a standing, queryable review agent that spawns fresh, cold reviewers per PR across the 13 dimensions and emits a fail-closed verdict. Build invokes it; an operator can invoke it directly. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-review-agent.md))
- **idc-review-coordinator**: The merged review engine coordinator — fans out the specialist reviewers, dedups + scores their findings, and emits the fail-closed verdict. ([llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow](https://github.com/llamallamaredpajama/idc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/idc-review-coordinator.md))

- **idea-incubator**: Synthesizes one concrete, refined next-iteration idea that directly targets the board's stated weaknesses. Dispatched by the idea-evolution orchestrator, never the judge of its own work. ([bhouvana/Insight](https://github.com/bhouvana/Insight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-incubator.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **idea-judge**: Lightweight blind scorer for the idea-tournament skill. Reads one packed brief file and writes the JSON it asks for to the path it names. Use for idea-tournament scoring rounds and head-to-heads. ([frank-shen-07/idea-tournament](https://github.com/frank-shen-07/idea-tournament/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-judge.md) · ★1)
- **idea-killer**: The harshest idea critic. Tries to kill your idea before the market does. Use when you need someone to find every flaw. ([c0x12c/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/c0x12c/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.codex/agents/idea-killer.md) · ★111 · 8 copies)

- **idea-mutator**: Produces one targeted mutation of a rejected idea, choosing the mutation operator that most directly addresses why it was rejected. Used internally by research-orchestrator, one rejected idea per invocation. ([gk-936/researcher-plugin](https://github.com/gk-936/researcher-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/idea-mutator.md))
- **idea-persona**: One simulated reviewer on the idea-team. Carries no persona of its own - the router injects a single persona card, the team rules, the facilitator's frame and (from round 2) the attributed digest of the previous round. Reviews an idea from that one lens, responds to named personas in later rounds, labels opinion as opinion and sources every fact claim. Dispatched by the idea-team skill in review and talk modes, one agent per persona per round, never sharing a context with another persona. ([P47Phoenix/Claude-Plugins](https://github.com/P47Phoenix/Claude-Plugins/blob/HEAD/bible-research/agents/idea-persona.md) · ★2)
- **idea-planner**: Senior architect that produces a written implementation plan for a change in the mission-deck repo. ([androszr/mission-deck](https://github.com/androszr/mission-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-planner.md))
- **idea-planner**: Fikir → PRD ajanı. CEO'nun iş fikrini ve mülakat cevaplarını alır, PRD dökümanını markdown olarak yazar ve Word (.docx) çıktısını üretir; revize notlarıyla aynı dökümanı günceller. - **idea-pressure-tester**: Adversarial stress-testing agent. Invoke when a research idea (IDEA.md) has passed Loop 1 and needs rigorous scrutiny before proceeding to method design. Evaluates four dimensions: novelty, assumption correctness, importance, and logical consistency. Performs independent adversarial literature searches. Produces a structured tester report and adversarial literature report. Does NOT fix or develop the idea — only finds problems. ([YurainSoon/research-director](https://github.com/YurainSoon/research-director/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/idea-pressure-tester.md))
- **idea-proposer**: Diversity-enforced hypothesis generator for the research loop.
